-- FIX: Make the UI execute and show normally without changing design

inst_3.Visible = true

-- Optional: simple toggle key (RightControl) to show/hide without altering visuals
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	if gameProcessed then return end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightControl then
		inst_3.Visible = not inst_3.Visible
	end
end)

print("[Vx7ModernV2] UI loaded successfully - Press RightControl to toggle")
